#Mother drunk/drugged during pregnancy, child is growing dumb.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.1
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy1_A
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drunk_birth } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy1_B
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drugged_birth } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_birth

	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_alive = yes
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			OR = { host = { character = ROOT } at_location = ROOT guardian = { character = ROOT } }
			OR = { trait = slow trait = imbecile }
		}
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Concerned, try stopping the bad habit if still into it.
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy1_A #Still drinking
			trigger = { trait = drunkard FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drunk_birth } }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy1_B #Still smoking
			trigger = {
				has_character_modifier = hashish_addict
				FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drugged_birth }
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy1_C #Stopped drinking
			trigger = {
				NOT = { trait = drunkard }
				FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drunk_birth }
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy1_D #Stopped smoking
			trigger = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hashish_addict }
				FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drugged_birth }
			}
		}
		tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes
		tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				father = {
					opinion = { modifier = opinion_irresponsible_mother who = ROOT years = 20 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = hashish_addict
				FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drugged_birth }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = withdrawal_symptoms } }
				add_character_modifier = { name = withdrawal_symptoms duration = 150 }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 20
				add_trait = stressed
				remove_character_modifier = hashish_addict
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_modifier = withdrawal_symptoms }
					remove_character_modifier = withdrawal_symptoms
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = drunkard FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drunk_birth } }
			random = { chance = 20 add_trait = stressed remove_trait = drunkard }
		}
	}
	option = { # Not concerned but still addicted.
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy1
		trigger = {
			NOT = { trait = kind }
			OR = {
				AND = { trait = drunkard FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drunk_birth } }
				AND = {
					has_character_modifier = hashish_addict
					FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drugged_birth }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	after = {
		FROM = { clr_character_flag = flag_drugged_birth clr_character_flag = flag_drunk_birth }
	}
}

#Father: Mother drunk/drugged during pregnancy, child is growing dumb.

	id = pregnancy.2
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy2_A
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drunk_birth } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy2_B
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_drugged_birth } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_birth

	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			is_alive = yes
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { trait = bastard }
			OR = { host = { character = ROOT } at_location = ROOT guardian = { character = ROOT } }
			OR = { trait = slow trait = imbecile }
		}
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy2_A #Still drinking
			trigger = {
				FROM = {
					mother = { trait = drunkard is_alive = yes }
					has_character_flag = flag_drunk_birth
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy2_B #Still smoking
			trigger = {
				FROM = {
					mother = { has_character_modifier = hashish_addict is_alive = yes }
					has_character_flag = flag_drugged_birth
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy2_C #Mother no longer addicted.
			trigger = {
				FROM = {
					mother = {
						is_alive = yes
						NOT = { trait = drunkard }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = hashish_addict }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy2_D #Mother died in the meantime
			trigger = { FROM = { mother = { is_alive = no } } }
		}
		FROM = {
			mother = {
				reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_irresponsible_mother who = ROOT years = 20 }
			}
		}
	}
}

	picture = GFX_evt_homage_hf

	trigger = {
		trait = pregnant
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		NOT = { trait = hard_pregnancy }
		NOT = { trait = troubled_pregnancy }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Bask in it
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy3
		tiered_prestige_reward_effect = yes
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = mother_pregnancy_glow duration = 80 }
		if = {
			limit = { is_main_spouse = yes }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				spouse = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.4 days = 15 random = 5 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 75
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = humble }
		}
	}

	option = { #Be humble about it
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy3
		if = {
			limit = { trait = proud }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				remove_trait = proud
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38300 } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = proud trait = humble } }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				add_trait = humble
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38260 } }
			}
		}
		else_if = { limit = { trait = humble } tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = proud }
		}
	}
}

						is_woman_trigger = yes
						is_adult = yes
						count = 2
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_ruler = yes
					any_courtier_or_vassal = {
						OR = { dynasty = FROM dynasty = ROOT }
						is_inaccessible_trigger = no
						NOT = { trait = incapable }
						NOT = { character = FROM }
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						prisoner = no
						is_woman_trigger = yes
						is_adult = yes
						count = 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
		prestige = 30
		piety = 10
		tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				show_scope_change = no
				add_character_modifier = { name = mother_comforted duration = 60 stacking = yes }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_swayed_2 years = 2 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				set_character_flag = flag_gossiping_relatives
				character_event = { id = pregnancy.6 days = 10 random = 15 } #Notification
			}
			if = { #If husband is ruler, pick from his court.
				limit = { is_ruler = yes }
				if = { # A player relative first...
					limit = {
						any_courtier_or_vassal = {
							OR = { dynasty = FROM dynasty = ROOT }
							is_inaccessible_trigger = no
							NOT = { trait = incapable }
							NOT = { character = FROM }
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							prisoner = no
							is_woman_trigger = yes
							is_adult = yes
							ai = no
						}
					}
					random_courtier_or_vassal = {
						limit = {
							OR = { dynasty = FROM dynasty = ROOT }
							is_inaccessible_trigger = no
							NOT = { trait = incapable }
							NOT = { character = FROM }
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							prisoner = no
							is_woman_trigger = yes
							is_adult = yes
							ai = no
						}
						character_event = { id = pregnancy.10 days = 5 random = 5 }
					}
				}
				else = { # ...otherwise an AI one.
					random_courtier_or_vassal = {
						limit = {
							OR = { dynasty = FROM dynasty = ROOT }
							is_inaccessible_trigger = no
							NOT = { trait = incapable }
							NOT = { character = FROM }
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							prisoner = no
							is_woman_trigger = yes
							is_adult = yes
							ai = yes
						}
						character_event = { id = pregnancy.10 days = 5 random = 5 }
					}
				}
			}
			else = { #If husband is not ruler, pick from her court.
				FROM = {
					if = { # A player relative first...
						limit = {
							any_courtier_or_vassal = {
								OR = { dynasty = FROM dynasty = ROOT }
								is_inaccessible_trigger = no
								NOT = { trait = incapable }
								NOT = { character = FROM }
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								prisoner = no
								is_woman_trigger = yes
								is_adult = yes
								ai = no
							}
						}
						random_courtier_or_vassal = {
							limit = {
								OR = { dynasty = FROM dynasty = ROOT }
								is_inaccessible_trigger = no
								NOT = { trait = incapable }
								NOT = { character = FROM }
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								prisoner = no
								is_woman_trigger = yes
								is_adult = yes
								ai = no
							}
							character_event = { id = pregnancy.10 days = 5 random = 5 }
						}
					}
					else = { # ...otherwise an AI one.
						random_courtier_or_vassal = {
							limit = {
								OR = { dynasty = FROM dynasty = ROOT }
								is_inaccessible_trigger = no
								NOT = { trait = incapable }
								NOT = { character = FROM }
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								prisoner = no
								is_woman_trigger = yes
								is_adult = yes
								ai = yes
							}
							character_event = { id = pregnancy.10 days = 5 random = 5 }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = adulteress } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = incestuous_adulteress } }
		}
	}
	option = { #Hire servants
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy5
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_pregnancy5_B }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.6 days = 10 random = 15 } } }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.05 min = -10 max = -75 }
		piety = 10
		tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				show_scope_change = no
				add_character_modifier = { name = mother_comforted duration = 60 stacking = yes }
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_swayed_2 years = 2 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = adulteress } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { has_character_modifier = incestuous_adulteress } }
		}
	}
	option = { #Do nothing
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy5
		piety = -10
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 3 FROM = { has_character_modifier = adulteress } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 FROM = { has_character_modifier = incestuous_adulteress } }
		}
	}
}

#Mother: gossiping has been organized.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.6
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy6_A
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_gossiping_relatives }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy6_B
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_gossiping_relatives } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_princess_byzantine

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		NOT = { trait = troubled_pregnancy }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy6
		trigger = { trait = gregarious }
		tooltip_info = gregarious
		if = { limit = { trait = depressed } remove_trait = depressed }
		add_character_modifier = { name = mother_comforted duration = 60 stacking = yes }
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_swayed_2 years = 2 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy6
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
		add_character_modifier = { name = mother_comforted duration = 60 stacking = yes }
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_swayed_2 years = 2 }
	}
	after = {
		clr_character_flag = flag_gossiping_relatives
		hidden_tooltip = {
			#Fallback events if the mother either has no relative or if the random relative pinged either refused the gossiping or died in the meantime.
			random_list = {
				#Mother enjoys herself, either becomes gregarious or trusting (patient offers chance for +1 learning).
				45 = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.7 days = 20 random = 5 } }
				#Mother not enjoying herself, either becomes envious or shy (deceitful offers chance for +1 intrigue).
				10 = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.8 days = 20 random = 5 } }
				#Mother pampered, either becomes content or gluttonous (diligent offers chance for +1 diplomacy)
				45 = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.9 days = 20 random = 5 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

#Mother: enjoying the gossiping.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.7
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy7_A
	picture = GFX_evt_princess_byzantine

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		NOT = { trait = troubled_pregnancy }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = pregnancy_gossip_cooldown }
	}

	option = { #Listen and learn
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy7
		trigger = { trait = patient }
		tooltip_info = patient
		change_learning = 1
		ai_chance = { factor = 80 }
	}
	option = { #Becoming gregarious
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy7
		if = {
			limit = { trait = shy }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = shy
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38295 } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOR = { trait = shy trait = gregarious } }
				random = {
					chance = 50
					add_trait = gregarious
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38265 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { trait = gregarious }
					random = { chance = 50 change_diplomacy = 1 }
				}
				else = { ai_chance = { factor = 50 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Relaxed and trusting
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy7
		if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = paranoid }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = paranoid
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38305 } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOR = { trait = trusting trait = paranoid } }
				random = {
					chance = 50
					add_trait = trusting
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38250 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { trait = trusting } random = { chance = 50 change_diplomacy = 1 } }
				else = { ai_chance = { factor = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

#Mother: pampered during gossiping.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.9
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy9_A
	picture = GFX_evt_princess_byzantine

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		NOT = { trait = troubled_pregnancy }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = pregnancy_gossip_cooldown }
	}

	option = { #Do your part
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy9
		trigger = { trait = diligent }
		tooltip_info = diligent
		change_stewardship = 1
		ai_chance = { factor = 80 }
	}
	option = { #Becoming content
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy9
		if = { limit = { trait = ambitious } random = { chance = 50 remove_trait = ambitious } }
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOR = { trait = content trait = ambitious } }
				random = {
					chance = 50
					add_trait = content
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38279 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { trait = content } random = { chance = 50 change_stewardship = 1 } }
				else = { ai_chance = { factor = 40 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Becoming gluttonous
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy9
		if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = temperate }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = temperate
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38276 } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOR = { trait = temperate trait = gluttonous } }
				random = {
					chance = 50
					add_trait = gluttonous
					gain_weight_small_effect = yes
					weight_trait_check_effect = yes
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38277 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { trait = gluttonous }
					random = {
						chance = 50
						change_diplomacy = 1
						gain_weight_medium_effect = yes
						weight_trait_check_effect = yes
					}
				}
				else = { ai_chance = { factor = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

#Relative: you have been invited to the gossiping.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.10
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy10_A
	picture = GFX_evt_princess_byzantine
	show_from_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROMFROM = {
			is_alive = yes
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { trait = troubled_pregnancy }
		}
	}

	option = { #Very well
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy10
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				90 = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.11 days = 12 random = 5 } } #Gossip imput
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 num_of_friends = 3 }
					modifier = { factor = 0 is_friend = FROMFROM }
					modifier = { factor = 0 is_rival = FROMFROM }
					modifier = { factor = 0 is_lover = FROMFROM }
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { opinion = { who = FROMFROM value = -30 } } }
					modifier = { factor = 0 FROMFROM = { num_of_friends = 3 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = kind }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = charitable }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 FROMFROM = { trait = shy } }
					FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.16 days = 12 random = 5 } } #Becoming friends with mother.
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 80 }
	}
	option = { #No time for it.
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy10
		trigger = { OR = { ai = no trait = shy trait = cruel } }
		piety = -5
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_disappointed years = 5 }
		}
		FROMFROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_offended years = 5 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
}

#Relative: gossiping input.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.11
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy11_A
	picture = GFX_evt_princess_byzantine
	show_from_from_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROMFROMFROM = {
			is_alive = yes
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { trait = troubled_pregnancy }
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		FROMFROMFROM = {
			add_character_modifier = { #Prevents the solo-events from firing instead of these.
				name = pregnancy_gossip_cooldown
				hidden = yes
				duration = 60
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Be talkative
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy11
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_evt20011_A }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.12 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 is_rival = FROMFROMFROM }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = socializer }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = shrewd }
		}
	}
	option = { #Be supportive
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy11
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_evt20011_B }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.13 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 is_rival = FROMFROMFROM }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 is_rival = FROMFROMFROM }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = dull }
		}
	}
	option = { #Be mean
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy11
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_evt20011_C }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.14 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 is_friend = FROMFROMFROM }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 is_lover = FROMFROMFROM }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 is_rival = FROMFROMFROM }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = stubborn }
		}
	}
}

				if = {
					limit = { trait = gregarious }

				}

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.15 } } }
	} #Notification to relative
}

			else = {
				if = { limit = { trait = content } random = { chance = 50 change_stewardship = 1 } }
				else = { ai_chance = { factor = 40 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Becoming gluttonous
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy9
		set_character_flag = mother_gossip_gluttonous
		if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = temperate }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = temperate
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38276 } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOR = { trait = temperate trait = gluttonous } }
				random = {
					chance = 50
					add_trait = gluttonous
					gain_weight_small_effect = yes
					weight_trait_check_effect = yes
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38277 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { trait = gluttonous }
					random = {
						chance = 50
						change_diplomacy = 1
						gain_weight_medium_effect = yes
						weight_trait_check_effect = yes
					}
				}
				else = { ai_chance = { factor = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.15 } } }
	} #Notification to relative
}

character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.19
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy19_A

	}

					modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_flag = flag_churching_piety }

#Response
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.20
	desc = { text = EVTDESCpregnancy20_A trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_vision } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy20_B
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_positive }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy20_C
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_negative }
	}
	desc = { #As she chokes, mother calls out for real father's name
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy20_D
		trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_sinner }
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_woman_praying_hf

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = flag_churching_wealth
		clr_character_flag = flag_churching_prestige
		clr_character_flag = flag_churching_piety
		clr_character_flag = flag_churching_nothing
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy20_A
			trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_vision }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy20_B
			trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_positive }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy20_C
			trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_negative }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy20_D
			trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_sinner }
		}

		#Cleansing resulted in very positive vision:
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_vision }
			tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
			if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
			if = {
				limit = { trait = cynical }
				random = {
					chance = 30
					remove_trait = cynical
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38316 } }
				}
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				spouse = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.21 } } #Notify husband
				event_target:newborn_baby = {
					character_event = { id = pregnancy.27 days = 2500 random = 250 }
				} #Child is blessed
			}
		}
		#Cleansing resulted in positive feeling:
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_positive }
			tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
			hidden_tooltip = {
				spouse = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.21 } } #Notify husband
			}
		}
		#Cleansing resulted in negative feeling:
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_negative }
			tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
				random = { chance = 40 add_trait = stressed }
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				spouse = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.21 } } #Notify husband
			}
		}
		#Cleansing resulted in very negative omen:
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_sinner }
			tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes
			if = { limit = { trait = stressed NOT = { trait = paranoid } } add_trait = paranoid }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } } add_trait = stressed }
			event_target:newborn_baby = { prestige = -100 }

			hidden_tooltip = {
				if = { #If child is not actually illegitimate
					limit = {
						event_target:newborn_baby = {
							real_father_even_if_dead = {
								event_target:newborn_baby = {
									father_even_if_dead = { character = PREVPREV }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					event_target:newborn_baby = {
						real_father_even_if_dead = {
							event_target:newborn_baby = { set_father = 0 }
						}
					}
				}
				if = { #If child is actually illegitimate
					limit = {
						event_target:newborn_baby = {
							real_father_even_if_dead = {
								event_target:newborn_baby = {
									father_even_if_dead = { NOT = { character = PREVPREV } }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					event_target:newborn_baby = {
						real_father_even_if_dead = {
							character_event = { id = pregnancy.25 } #Notify real father
							event_target:newborn_baby = { set_father = PREV }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = { #If spouse is higher tier, the child is no longer an heir.
				limit = { spouse = { higher_tier_than = ROOT } }
				event_target:newborn_baby = { add_trait = bastard dynasty = mother_bastard }
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				spouse = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.21 } } #Notify husband
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.26 days = 30 } } #Clear all flags
	}
}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy21_A
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_vision } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy21_B
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_positive } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy21_C
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_negative } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy21_D
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_sinner } }
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_woman_praying_hf

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_married = ROOT is_alive = yes }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Single option because the Cleansing was positive.
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = flag_churching_vision
					has_character_flag = flag_churching_positive
				}
			}
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy21_A
			trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_vision } }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTApregnancy21_B
			trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_positive } }
		}
		#Cleansing resulted in very positive vision:
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_vision } }
			tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
			if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
			if = { limit = { trait = depressed } remove_trait = depressed }
		}
		#Cleansing resulted in positive feeling:
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_positive }
			tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
			if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Cleansing was negative, high diplomacy offers comfort
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy21
		trigger = { diplomacy = 10 FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_negative } }
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.22 } } #Spouse comforted.
			if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
			opinion = { modifier = comforted_opinion who = ROOT years = 2 }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = cruel } random = { chance = 30 remove_trait = cruel } }
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOR = { trait = cruel trait = kind } }
				random = {
					chance = 30
					add_trait = kind
					hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { trait = kind } tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes }
				else = { ai_chance = { factor = 100 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Cleansing was negative
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy21
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_negative } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
	option = { #Cleansing was horrible, but husband forgives wife.
		name = EVTOPTDpregnancy21
		trigger = {
			FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_sinner }
			NOR = { trait = cruel trait = envious }
		}
		FROM = {
			if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
			opinion = { modifier = comforted_opinion who = ROOT years = 10 }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.23 } } #Spouse forgiven.
		}
		tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = kind
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = patient }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = humble }
		}
	}
	option = { #Cleansing was horrible, and husband is outraged.
		name = EVTOPTEpregnancy21
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = flag_churching_sinner } }
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.24 } } #Spouse denounced.
			if = { limit = { higher_tier_than = FROM } tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes }
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = adulteress years = 10 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = zealous }
		}
	}
}

				remove_trait = dull
				remove_trait = imbecile
				remove_trait = slow
				remove_trait = quick
				add_trait = genius
			}
			10 = {
				remove_trait = dull
				remove_trait = imbecile
				remove_trait = slow
				add_trait = quick
			}
			10 = { remove_trait = ugly add_trait = beautiful }
			15 = { remove_trait = weak remove_trait = feeble add_trait = strong }
			20 = { remove_trait = ugly add_trait = fair }
			20 = {
				remove_trait = dull
				remove_trait = imbecile
				remove_trait = slow
				add_trait = shrewd
			}
			20 = { remove_trait = weak remove_trait = feeble add_trait = robust }
		}
		mother = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.28 } }
		father = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.28 } }
	}
}

#Dark rest. Fires for female rulers, or, if unlanded, for their spouse.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.30
	desc = { text = EVTDESCpregnancy30_A trigger = { trait = pregnant } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCpregnancy30_B trigger = { NOT = { trait = pregnant } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_woman_praying_hf

	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			trait = pregnant
			is_alive = yes
			OR = { is_married = ROOT character = ROOT }
			NOR = {
				trait = incapable
				trait = troubled_pregnancy
				trait = on_hajj
				trait = on_pilgrimage
				trait = on_indian_pilgrimage
				trait = kow_tow_travels
				trait = travelling
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Resting in the dark
		name = { text = EVTOPTApregnancy30_A trigger = { trait = pregnant } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTApregnancy30_B trigger = { NOT = { trait = pregnant } } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = pregnant } }
			FROM = { #Wife scope
				add_character_modifier = { modifier = mother_dark_rest months = 5 }
				set_character_flag = flag_mother_dark_rest #Reduces chance of child being born sickly.
				hidden_tooltip = {
					random_list = {
						25 = {
							character_event = { id = pregnancy.31 days = 10 random = 10 } #Becomes learned
						}
						25 = {
							modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }
							character_event = { id = pregnancy.33 days = 10 random = 10 } #Becomes paranoid
						}
						25 = {
							modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = gregarious }
							character_event = { id = pregnancy.35 days = 10 random = 10 } #Becomes calmer
						}
						25 = { #Nothing
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = pregnant }
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = mother_dark_rest months = 5 }
			set_character_flag = flag_mother_dark_rest
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						character_event = { id = pregnancy.31 days = 10 random = 10 } #Becomes learned
					}
					25 = {
						modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = shy }
						character_event = { id = pregnancy.33 days = 10 random = 10 } #Becomes paranoid
					}
					25 = {
						modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = gregarious }
						character_event = { id = pregnancy.35 days = 10 random = 10 } #Becomes calmer
					}
					25 = { #Nothing
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = wroth }
		}
	}
	option = { #Talking
		name = { text = EVTOPTBpregnancy30_A trigger = { trait = pregnant } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTBpregnancy30_B trigger = { NOT = { trait = pregnant } } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = pregnant } }
			FROM = { #Wife scope
				add_character_modifier = { modifier = mother_chatting months = 5 }
				hidden_tooltip = {
					random_list = {
						35 = {
							character_event = { id = pregnancy.37 days = 10 random = 10 } #Becomes more gregarious
						}
						35 = {
							character_event = { id = pregnancy.39 days = 10 random = 10 } #Becomes more popular
						}
						5 = {
							modifier = {
								factor = 0
								NOR = {
									trait = kind
									has_focus = focus_family
									trait = socializer
									trait = gregarious
								}
							}
							modifier = {
								factor = 0
								ROOT = {
									NOR = {
										trait = kind
										has_focus = focus_family
										trait = socializer
										trait = gregarious
									}
								}
							}
							modifier = { factor = 0 ROOT = { num_of_lovers = 1 } }
							modifier = { factor = 0 prefers_men_trigger = yes }
							modifier = { factor = 0 ROOT = { prefers_men_trigger = no } }
							modifier = { factor = 0 is_main_spouse = no }
							modifier = { factor = 0 num_of_lovers = 1 }

						}

				}

		}
		if = {

			hidden_tooltip = {

									trait = gregarious
								}

						modifier = { factor = 0 num_of_lovers = 1 }

#Pagan festival of fertility
#Only for Count or higher married rulers (or their main spouse).
#Only for Norse, Finnish, Romuva.
#Goddesses mentioned for loc: Freyja (norse), Zemyna (Romuva/baltic), Akka (Finnish)

#Spouse: organize fertility festival for wife.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.43
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy43_A
	picture = GFX_evt_viking_throneroom_oldgods
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		FROM = {
			trait = pregnant
			is_alive = yes
			is_married = ROOT
			OR = { #Can be of different but similar religion.
				religion = norscan_gods
				religion = norscan_gods_reformed
				religion = old_faith
				religion = northern_gods
				religion = amazon
				religion = anath_raema
				religion = kukaili
			}
			NOR = {
				trait = incapable
				has_character_modifier = out_traveling
				trait = travelling
				trait = troubled_pregnancy
				has_character_modifier = mother_fertility_festival_1
				has_character_modifier = mother_fertility_festival_2
				trait = on_hajj
				trait = on_pilgrimage
				trait = on_indian_pilgrimage
				trait = kow_tow_travels
				trait = travelling
			}
		}
		OR = {
			religion = norscan_gods
			religion = norscan_gods_reformed
			religion = old_faith
			religion = northern_gods
			religion = amazon
			religion = anath_raema
			religion = kukaili
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Organize full festival.
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy43
		if = { limit = { ai = no } scaled_wealth = { value = -0.25 min = -25 max = -100 } }
		tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
		spouse = {
			show_scope_change = no
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = mother_fertility_festival_2 years = 4 }
		}
		spouse = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_swayed_2 years = 4 }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.45 } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { wealth = 25 } }
		}
	}
	option = { #Give proper offerings
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy43
		if = { limit = { ai = no } scaled_wealth = { value = -0.05 min = -5 max = -50 } }
		spouse = {
			show_scope_change = no
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = mother_fertility_festival_1 years = 2 }
		}
		spouse = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_swayed_1 years = 2 }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.44 } } #Notify wife, end line.
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { wealth = 0 } }
		}
	}
	option = { #Not interested.
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy43
		tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
}

#Mother: festival has been organized for you.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.45
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy45_A
	picture = GFX_evt_viking_throneroom_oldgods
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Excited
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy45
		trigger = { OR = { trait = gregarious trait = proud trait = socializer trait = hedonist } }
		set_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_excited
		tiered_prestige_reward_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { #Flattered
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy45
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = { #Humbled
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy45
		trigger = { OR = { trait = shy trait = chaste trait = humble } }
		set_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_humbled
		tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}

	after = {
		tooltip = {
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = mother_fertility_festival_2 years = 4 }
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_swayed_2 years = 4 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				20 = { # Mother of the realm
					modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_excited }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charitable }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = proud }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = envious }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = cruel }
					character_event = { id = pregnancy.46 days = 5 random = 5 }
				}
				20 = { # A goddess am I
					modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_humbled }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = envious }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = cruel }
					modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = humble }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = shy }
					character_event = { id = pregnancy.47 days = 5 random = 5 }
				}
				10 = { # The people rejoice
					modifier = { factor = 1.15 trait = charitable }
					modifier = { factor = 1.15 trait = gregarious }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_attractive_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_unattractive_trigger = yes }
					character_event = { id = pregnancy.49 days = 5 random = 5 }
				}
				10 = { # Vassals are happy
					modifier = { factor = 1.15 trait = kind }
					modifier = { factor = 1.15 trait = gregarious }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_attractive_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_unattractive_trigger = yes }
					character_event = { id = pregnancy.50 days = 5 random = 5 }
				}
				20 = { # Avatar of fertility
					modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = celibate }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = hedonist }
					modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = temperate }
					modifier = { factor = 0.1 age = 40 }
					character_event = { id = pregnancy.52 days = 5 random = 5 }
				}
				20 = { # The virtue of the goddess
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.25
						NOR = { trait = lustful trait = chaste trait = celibate }
					}
					character_event = { id = pregnancy.53 days = 5 random = 5 }
				}
			}
			clr_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_excited
			clr_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_humbled
		}
	}
}

	id = pregnancy.46

	picture = GFX_evt_homage_hf
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Gregarious
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy46
		if = { limit = { trait = gregarious } change_diplomacy = 1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = gregarious } } add_trait = gregarious }
		if = { limit = { trait = shy } remove_trait = shy }
		set_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_gregarious
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { #Kind
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy46
		if = { limit = { trait = kind } change_diplomacy = 1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }
		if = { limit = { trait = envious } remove_trait = envious }
		if = { limit = { trait = cruel } remove_trait = cruel }
		set_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_kind
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { #Piety
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy46
		tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
		set_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_piety
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_married = yes }
				spouse = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.54 } } #Spouse notified
			}
			else = {
				clr_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_gregarious
				clr_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_kind
				clr_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_piety
			}
		}
	}
}

		if = { limit = { trait = humble } remove_trait = humble }
		set_character_flag = flag_mother_festival_proud
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Ambitious
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy47
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } } add_trait = ambitious }
		if = { limit = { trait = content } remove_trait = content }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				add_trait = lunatic_1
				set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 5 }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.48 } }
			}

		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }

			}

#Hollow child gains specific trait.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.57
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy57_A
	picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
	hide_from = yes

	has_character_flag = flag_hollow_child_primed

	min_age = 7
	max_age = 10

	trigger = {
		trait = is_malnourished
		NOR = {
			has_nickname = nick_the_pale_prince
			has_nickname = nick_the_hollow
			has_character_flag = flag_hollow_child
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy57
		if = { limit = { OR = { trait = brooding trait = envious } } change_intrigue = 2 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = brooding } has_dlc = "Conclave" } add_trait = brooding }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = brooding } NOT = { has_dlc = "Conclave" } }
			add_trait = envious
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 80 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy57
		if = { limit = { OR = { trait = timid trait = shy } } change_intrigue = 2 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = timid } has_dlc = "Conclave" } add_trait = timid }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = timid } NOT = { has_dlc = "Conclave" } } add_trait = shy }
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	after = {
		ROOT = {
			clr_character_flag = flag_hollow_child_primed
			set_character_flag = flag_hollow_child
			if = { limit = { has_minor_title = title_prince } give_nickname = nick_the_pale_prince }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_minor_title = title_prince } }
				give_nickname = nick_the_hollow
			}
		}
	}
}

					has_character_modifier = incestuous_adulteress
				}
			}
		}
		opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_executed_child years = 10 }

		FROM = {
			tooltip = {
				add_character_modifier = { modifier = cowardly_infanticide years = 100 }
				tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
			}
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.62 } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 80
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 religion_group = old_world_group trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = paranoid }
		}
	}
	option = { #Forgive her.
		name = EVTOPTCpregnancy61
		trigger = {
			NOT = { trait = cruel }
			FROM = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_modifier = adulteress
					has_character_modifier = incestuous_adulteress
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_forgiven years = 10 }
		}
		tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = pregnancy.63 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = trusting }
		}
	}
	option = { #Do not care.
		name = EVTOPTDpregnancy61
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_modifier = adulteress
					has_character_modifier = incestuous_adulteress
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

#Mother becomes depressed/insane after abortion.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.64
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy64_A
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy64
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = depressed
				OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			}
			health = -1.0
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = depressed
				NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			}
			add_trait = lunatic_1
			set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 5 }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } } add_trait = depressed }
	}
}

#Notify lover that his lover is pregnant.

	id = pregnancy.67
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { OR = { is_married = no is_married = ROOT } } }
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy67_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { FROM = { is_married = yes NOT = { is_married = ROOT } } }
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy67_B
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_pregnancy

	trigger = {
		FROM = {
			NOT = { is_married = ROOT } #Otherwise double notification.
		}
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTApregnancy67
		trigger = { FROM = { OR = { is_married = no is_married = ROOT } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBpregnancy67
		trigger = { FROM = { is_married = yes NOT = { is_married = ROOT } } }
	}
}

#Cleanup on_birth event to remove illegitimate trait gained when a wife gives birth after the death of her husband.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.68
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOR = {
			mother_even_if_dead = { has_character_modifier = adulteress }
			mother_even_if_dead = { has_character_modifier = incestuous_adulteress }
			father_even_if_dead = { is_alive = yes }
			father_even_if_dead = { trait = eunuch }
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		remove_trait = bastard
		remove_trait = legit_bastard
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The firstborn (pregnancy.100-102)
###############################################################################

# pregnancy.100 A first child or first son
# Hidden. A ruler parent reacts to their first child (101) or first son (102).
# Scope: ROOT is the newborn. Fired by the on_birth random list.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.100

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	only_playable = yes

	option = {
		mother = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_ruler = yes dynasty = ROOT NOT = { num_of_children = 2 } }
				character_event = { id = pregnancy.101 days = 7 }
			}
		}
		father = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_ruler = yes dynasty = ROOT NOT = { num_of_children = 2 } }
				character_event = { id = pregnancy.101 days = 7 }
			}
		}

		#Finally a son
		mother = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler = yes

					#First male will inherit
					NOT = { primary_title = { has_law = true_cognatic_succession } }
					OR = {
						primary_title = { has_law = succ_primogeniture }
						primary_title = { has_law = succ_gavelkind }
					}

					#Child is legitimate and not firsborn
					dynasty = ROOT
					ROOT = { NOT = { trait = bastard } }
					ROOT = { is_female = no }
					num_of_children = 2

					#There is no other legitimate son
					NOT = {
						any_child = {
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							is_female = no
							dynasty = ROOT
							NOT = { trait = bastard }
						}
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = pregnancy.102 days = 7 }
			}
		}

		father = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler = yes

					#First male will inherit
					NOT = { primary_title = { has_law = true_cognatic_succession } }
					OR = {
						primary_title = { has_law = succ_primogeniture }
						primary_title = { has_law = succ_gavelkind }
					}

					#Child is legitimate and not firsborn
					dynasty = ROOT
					ROOT = { NOT = { trait = bastard } }
					ROOT = { is_female = no }
					num_of_children = 2

					#There is no other legitimate son
					NOT = {
						any_child = {
							NOT = { character = ROOT }
							is_female = no
							dynasty = ROOT
							NOT = { trait = bastard }
						}
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = pregnancy.102 days = 7 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# pregnancy.101 The firstborn
# The parent sees their first child.
# Scope: ROOT is the parent, FROM the child. Fired by pregnancy.100.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.101
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_birth

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_child_of = ROOT }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = good_relationship_omen }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = bad_relationship_omen }
	}

	immediate = {
		#Child is ugly
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = ugly } } set_character_flag = text_ugly break = yes }

		#Child is weak/sickly/inbred
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { OR = { trait = sickly trait = inbred trait = weak } } }
			set_character_flag = text_weak
			break = yes
		}

		#Child is strong
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = strong } } set_character_flag = text_strong break = yes }

		#Child is bastard
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = bastard } }
			set_character_flag = text_bastard
			break = yes
		}
		#Female won't inherit
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				OR = {
					primary_title = { has_law = agnatic_succession }
					primary_title = { has_law = succ_patrician_elective }
					religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = text_girl_cant_inherit
			break = yes
		}
		#Firstborn will inherit
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							is_woman_trigger = yes
							ROOT = { primary_title = { has_law = true_cognatic_succession } }
						}
						is_female = no
					}
				}
				OR = { has_law = succ_primogeniture has_law = succ_gavelkind }
			}
			set_character_flag = text_firstborn_inherits
			break = yes
		}
		#All other outcomes
		set_character_flag = text_unsure_future
	}

	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = text_bastard } text = EVTDESCpregnancy101_A }
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = text_girl_cant_inherit }
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy101_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = text_firstborn_inherits }
		text = EVTDESCpregnancy101_C
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = text_unsure_future } text = EVTDESCpregnancy101_D }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = text_ugly } text = EVTDESCpregnancy101_E }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = text_weak } text = EVTDESCpregnancy101_F }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = text_strong } text = EVTDESCpregnancy101_G }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = text_bastard } text = EVTOPTpregnancy101_A_A }
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = text_girl_cant_inherit }
			text = EVTOPTpregnancy101_A_B
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = text_firstborn_inherits }
			text = EVTOPTpregnancy101_A_C
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { has_character_flag = text_unsure_future }
			text = EVTOPTpregnancy101_A_A
		}
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = text_ugly } text = EVTOPTpregnancy101_A_D }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = text_weak } text = EVTOPTpregnancy101_A_E }
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = text_strong } text = EVTOPTpregnancy101_A_F }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = text_firstborn_inherits }
				FROM = { prestige = 50 }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = text_firstborn_inherits
					has_character_flag = text_strong
				}
				NOT = { FROM = { mother = { character = ROOT } } }
			}

			FROM = { mother = { save_event_target_as = firstborn_mother } }

			opinion = {
				who = event_target:firstborn_mother
				modifier = opinion_mother_of_my_child
				years = 5
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = text_bastard
		clr_character_flag = text_girl_cant_inherit
		clr_character_flag = text_firstborn_inherits
		clr_character_flag = text_unsure_future
		clr_character_flag = text_ugly
		clr_character_flag = text_weak
		clr_character_flag = text_strong
	}
}

# pregnancy.102 A son at last
# Relief at a first son and heir.
# Scope: ROOT is the parent, FROM the child. Fired by pregnancy.100.
character_event = {
	id = pregnancy.102
	desc = EVTDESCpregnancy102
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_birth

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		FROM = { prestige = 50 }
		name = EVTOPTpregnancy101_A_C

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { FROM = { mother = { character = ROOT } } } }

			FROM = { mother = { save_event_target_as = firstborn_mother } }

			opinion = {
				who = event_target:firstborn_mother
				modifier = opinion_mother_of_my_child
				years = 5
			}
		}
	}
}
